1a1(kha skong): @#//rgyal ba'i bka' 'gyur rin po che sna thang par ma'i kha skong phyogs gcig tu sdebs pa bzhugs so//
2b1(kha skong): lta bu zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs te/sku dang ye shes thams cad la khyab par bya ba'i phyir/sku mdog dkar po zhal drug 
2b2(kha skong): dang ldan par byas nas/'jig rten pa thams cad la gzigs nas/rang gi rigs kyi yi ge nyid brjod pas sku dang gsung dang 
2b3(kha skong): thugs dang yon tan dang phrin las dang ye shes kyang gcig rigs ngo bo sprul pa'i skur gyur to//kye ma bcom ldan rdo rje che//don rnams 
2b4(kha skong): bskyed pa yang nyid do//mkha' dkyil 'od kyis 'gengs 'byung ba//slar zhing kha dog lha ldan pa//sa bon sngags kyis bskul ba 
2b5(kha skong): yi//'od kyi rtse phran thub pa'i sku//kun kyang sangs rgyas gnas las skyes//rang la zhugs pas sku rdzogs gyur//zhal drug khro gnyer 
2b6(kha skong): rab brjid pa//ma he'i zhal ldan steng gnas pa//'jam dpal zhal ni 'dzum bcas bzhugs//lhag ma dkar dang ser dang 
3a1(kha skong): @#//dmar//du ba'i mdog mtshungs gyen skrar ldan//thod pa skam rlon rgyu ma sprul//rus pa rdo rje'i phreng bas brgyan//glang chen stag 
3a2(kha skong): lpags gyon nas ni//hUM phaT glur len gad mo can//nam mkha'i lus can bre ba'i sprin//yang dang yang du smyo byed pa'i//phyag drug rab tu brjid 
3a3(kha skong): pa yi//dbyug pa thob gtun shing dag/g.yas pa'i phyag gis rim bzhin 'dzin//dgra sta dang ni lcags kyu dang //zhags pa g.yon pa'i 
3a4(kha skong): phyag gis 'dzin//pad+ma nyi ma ma he dang //gshin rje'i steng na 'gying bag can//zhabs drug g.yon brkyang gar gyis gnas//'jig rten bdag po mthar mdzad pa'o//
3a5(kha skong): de ltar gang zhig dad pa dang //blo ldan g.yo sgyu rab spangs shing //legs par dbang rnams thob byas nas//rdo rje slob dpon mnyes byed pas//
3a6(kha skong): yang nas yang du dran byed na//brtan pa chen po thob par 'gyur//lha yi dge 'dun nga la nyon//dang po chang la rab dga' zhing //sha chen 
3b1(kha skong): rtag tu mur byed na//las rnams de yis myur du 'grub//sku ni du ma 'phro byed cing //sku la sku rnams nges thim byed//thams cad sgyu ma nyid shes pas//'jig rten rnams su yid sad byed//khong stong kha dog 
3b2(kha skong): gar ba ste//rgyun tu rlung ni 'dzag par byed//chu skyes dmar po kha sbyar gnyis//yan lag gnyis kyis legs par bcun//shes pa 'phel ba me dang rlung //nyi ma zla ba thabs ldan dang //shes rab ldan par shes par 
3b3(kha skong): bya//gnyis po de nyid rnam spangs na//khong du chud par mi 'gyur ro//chu skyes kha sbyor la brten te//gsum gnas pa yi ting 'dzin la//rdo rje chos kyis btab pa yis//thabs dang shes rab grol ba'i rgyun blo ldan 
3b4(kha skong): 'das pa shes par 'gyur//de phyir de la brten par bya//chos rnams chos nyid dbyer med pas//stong pa nyid kyi ye shes che//mi shigs rtog bral mtshan ma med//bde chen ye shes rdo rje smug/ye shes ma 
3b5(kha skong): rtog rtog pa bya ba//rtog pa'i mtshan ma thams cad sreg/spros pa'i mtshan ma mtha' dag ni//ji snyed sgyu ma'i rnam par shes//de nyid shes nas grol ba'o//ma shes pas ni phyir zhing 'khor//'jig rten 
3b6(kha skong): skye bo blo ldan 'das//nges par rtogs pa thabs kyi mchog/sdug bsngal chen pos rab 'jigs nas//bde ba 'dod pas nan tan bya//dpal rdo rje 'jigs byed kyi rtog pa'i rgyud kyi rgyal po las/thabs dang shes rab kyi 
3b7(kha skong): bsam gtan bstan pa'i le'u ste dang po'o////
4a1(kha skong): @#//sbyar||zhi sogs bzlas pa'i rim pa drug/rdo rje'i dbus su rdo rje ni//nyi shu la brten yi ge yi//phreng ba kha dog lngar shes pa//skyen bu la spangs nas klag pa ni//mkhas pas 
4a2(kha skong): bzlas pa'i rim pa'o//drag pa'i las rnams sgrub pa la//nyon mongs bzo ba'i 'khor lo ni//sngon po rtsi rjibs bcu pa la//'od zer sna tshogs 'phro ba bsam//zhi bar 'dod pa'o skyes bu des//
4a3(kha skong): sems ni yang dag mnyam bzhag nas//sngags kyi 'od kyang 'gro gzhan la//phan pa'i yid dang ldan par bya//gro ga ras dang shing shun la//gur gum gi waM dri rnams kyis//mchod rten rim pa lnga ldan 
4a4(kha skong): zhing||gdugs dang 'khor lo ldan par bya//sgrub pa po yi ming dang rus//de yi bum pa'i nang du bri//rten 'brel snying po la sogs pa'i//sngags kyis g.yon nas bskor ba bri//cig shos g.yas ras 
4a5(kha skong): g.yon du bri//de la spel tshig med pa 'am//yang na 'khor lo'i bar du bzhag/'khor lo kun la kha sbyor gnad//rab tu gnas par byas nas kyang //lus sam mgul lam gang bdar bzhag/des ni 
4a6(kha skong): gdon dang byad stems dang //rims dang yams nams sel bar byed//gang zhig yul rnams gzhom 'dod na//hUM gi sa phon dang ldan par//ming yig tsam po bri bar bya//sa phab/phyi ma med ros 
4a7(kha skong): dang||ba bam he'i khrag gis brdzis//zla gam gnyis pa'i gzugs byas te//bar du gang 'dod bsgrub pa yi//gzugs brnyan sor brgyad pa yi tshad//byas la gan rkyal du bzhag la//me tog gdong gi 
4b1(kha skong): sbyor ba yis//khri phrag gcig tu sngags bzlas nas//dum bu dum bur gtubs byas te//chu bo'i gzhung du gtor byas na//skad cig de la de dbang 'gyur//bya rog 'ug pa'i gzugs byas te//ma num li ro tsa nas//khyo 
4b2(kha skong): shugs bsgrub bya'i ming bris te//gshin rje'i phyogs bltas gsang sngags gdon//rkang pa g.yon pas mnan nas ni//mi yi rje ngar khur bus gdab//skad cig tsam la de dag dbye//de nas 'dir ni bsad pa yi//
4b3(kha skong): cho ga'i rim pa bshad par bya//zhal drug phyag drug zhabs drug pa'am//yang na zhal bzhi phyag bzhi pa'am//zhal gcig phyag gnyis pa yang rung //ma he gdong pa'i sbyor ba yis//brag rnams sna tshogs tha 
4b4(kha skong): dad dang //bsgrub bya'i rjes dang dug gi rigs//rta dang ma he bung bu'i chus//sdus pa'i bsgrub bya'i ming ga bya//lus la tsher mas gzugs bya zhing //til mar byis kyang bsku bar bya//yang na shing le gro ga'am//
4b5(kha skong): dang khrod ras sam shun pa la//rang nyid gcer bu smra ba'i gnas//lus ni thal bas phyogs byas ste//mngon sam khas nas sha chen bza'//rab tu zhim pa'i tshang ba bzhi//nag po'i tshes kyi mtshan phyed dus//
4b6(kha skong): 'khor lo dra bar btum par ba//ling che bcu gnyis 'khor lo ma bcad do//rtsi bzhin bya bas mtshan la//yangs gan til dang skar chu dang //rta dang ma he khyi phag khrag/bya rog 'ug pa'i sgro 
4b7(kha skong): yis bra//na yi g.yon pa dang //phyag dang rigs bskang bris//phyogs bzhir yid bcu bzhin de dang bcas pa bri bar bya//brgyad po dag la rim pa bzhin//yi ge 'di dang /bri bar bya//e ya ma 
5a1(kha skong): @#//dza kA la ru pa a mu ka mA ra ya hUM hUM phaT phaT swA hA//yi ge rnams la bskor nas ni//bsgrub bya gzugs dang ldan pa bri//de yi mu khyud dag la ni//yi ge bcu pa ming bcas bri//
5a2(kha skong): phyogs bzhir rtse gsum rdo rjes mtshan//de bzhin rdzas rnams ma lus pas//'khor lo lta bar ldan pa bri//gshin rje'i gzugs ni ral gri dang //dbyig pa 'dzin pa bris nas ni//de dang de nyid lto spyad la//
5a3(kha skong): mtshan ngan thod pa'i nang du gzhug/tsha gsum ske tshe dgu mu bri//ro nyes bskang nas dkyil 'khor ni//gru gsum dbus su bzhag par bya//yungs dang bra bos lu nag/rdo b+hai bra bo sogs rnams la//gsang 
5a4(kha skong): sngags rgyal po brjod byas te//bro'am du ba 'thul bar du//rim pa dang rim gyis khrag rnams ni//ma lus zad par 'thung ba yi//ma yengs par ni bsam gtan bya//yang na bsgrub bya za bar mthong //
5a5(kha skong): shing gcig rtsar ni rengs pa dang //mal 'og tu ni ma zhing lkugs//chu mdor byed cing ri rtse bskrad//bsad pa ma thab 'og tu sba//dbug pa la ni sgo khang 'og/lha 'am 'khor lo sgyur ba'o//bde gnas 
5a6(kha skong): rnams kyis dbang du 'gyur//nam bu chen po thod pa yi//nang du blugs nas bskal byas te//bzher bas mig la byug byed na//mngon shes de nyid myur du 'thob//rigs ldan rgyal po las rnyed cing //mdog 
5a7(kha skong): ldan lang tsho gsang ba 'dug la//tsan dan lde gu mang byas te//las kyi sbyor bas de nyid bcing //bar na me la gdung bas 'dug/oM badzra kro ta ya ma rA dzaH/badzra kI li kI la ha na ha na mA ra ya phaT/ces bya'i 
5b1(kha skong): gsang sngags brjod byas la//rus pa'i phur pa gdab par bya//gsal shis la btsugs skya ru yi//rdzas wa brten te bsgrub par bya//nyil mar byugs la thod pas bzhag/mang me byas pa'i dud pa yis//mig rnon dag ni 
5b2(kha skong): 'grub par 'gyur//gsang sngags rgyal po thogs med la//bsten pas thob bya ma spong seng //'di ni brjod pa re zhig dgos//yang na 'di ltar shes bya ste//rang nyid sngags dang ting 'dzin ldan//brtson chen gzhan du ma 
5b3(kha skong): yengs pas//gang du brtsams pa de 'grub 'gyur//smad 'tshong rang byung nyid kyis ni//sngon bstan tsakra legs bris te//ling ga'i snying khar bcug byas la//rab mdun khrag gi zla gam ste//khrag gis byugs pa'i steng 
5b4(kha skong): du bzhag/mi 'am rgyal po'i bu mo dag/dgug dang dbang gi yi ge yis//de nyid du ni ngas par 'gyur//nyal ba'i 'og tu mnan par bya//mi glang nga khyi beng phag khrag/'khor lo bris nas ling ga yi//mgrin par 
5b5(kha skong): bcug nas bya rog gi/khrag gis khrag skyug sngags bris nas//yi ge de ldan thod pa ru//bcug nas gsang sngags mang bzlas te//dur khrod me thab 'og bzhag na//khrag skyug ga la the tshom med//bsros na cha ba'i 
5b6(kha skong): nad kyi 'chi//sngal khrag pha wang klad pa yis//'khor lo de nyid legs bris nas//ling ga'i mchan khung g.yon bcug la//g.yas g.yon sbal khrag Ang khrag byugs//ling ga'i phyogs su rang nyid kyi//
5b7(kha skong): rkang pa g.yon pa'i brtse bos bcan//de tshe brdzas pa man bya ba yis//rgya gram lam du mnan la bzhag/de nas bzlog pa bris nas ni//shing gcig long bar sbas na rings//rnga mo bya rog rgan po'i khrag/'khor lo 
6a1(kha skong): @#//bris nas ling ga yi//rkang gnyis par bcug rang mdun du//khrag gis rnga mong gzugs bris te//de yi stong bzhag rang nyid kyis//rlung gi rtsas ma gtan nges ldan na//bskrad pa nyid du the 
6a2(kha skong): tshom med//ri bo rtse la 'dzegs te sba//ne'u le sbrul rta ma he dang //bya rog 'ug pa'i khrag gis ni//khyo shug rkang pa'i rjes sprus pa'i//ling ga sor brgyad par byas te//khrag des bris pa'i 'khor lo dag/snying 
6a3(kha skong): khar bcug pa gnyis byas te//ne'u le gzugs byas steng du bzhag/lag gnyis rta dang ma he yi//rus pas brdung zhing gsang sngags zlos//rtse drung shing khar sbas na 'byed//tsan+da dkar pos legs bris 
6a4(kha skong): nas||gser sogs rin chen rdzas su dril//mdun du zla ba'i gzugs steng du//bzhag pa nyin re bskor ba ni//ji srid byas nas bsrung bar nges//spyi bor btags na g.yul las rgyal//gang bar btags na 
6a5(kha skong): chu steng 'gro//ba yi 'o mas khrus byas te//ku sha ba mar sogs mchod nas//gsol ba ba nad rnams thams cad bsrung //de nas bcom ldan 'das las zla ba thams cad las rgyal ba zhes byung ba'i ri khrod cing 
6a6(kha skong): la snyoms par zhugs nas//las thams cad kyi rdzas su 'gro ba'i sngags 'di/sku dang gsung dang thugs rdo rje las phyung ngo//mA ra ya phaT/ces pa bsad pa'i 'o//bi dwe la yar bye ba'i 'o//uts+tshaT sa badzra da 
6a7(kha skong): pa'i 'o//mo ha la rmongs pa'i 'o//wa shaM ku ru dbang du bya ba'i 'o//sta ma ya mnan pa'i 'o//pA ka r+Na a joHdgug pa'i 'o//o hIHswAhA//kha dang lag pa bshal ba'i sngags so//o hAHSh+Ti a dza 
6b1(kha skong): kr-i tA na na hUM sarba sha trun nA sha la stam+b+ha ya phaT phaT swAhA/snying po'i sngags so//oM hrIHkA la rU pa hUM khaM/nye ba'i snying po'o//oM hrIHkruM puM s+yaM dzaM hUM ga/snying po dam pa'o//oM hrIHSh+T+rIHbo ma hA kro d+ha gats+tsha

6b3(kha skong): dzaHhUM phaT/ma tog 'bul ba'i sngags so//oM hriHhaHhe raN+Da/bdug pa'i'o//oM kA la rU pa sarba daSh+Tu satwa dam kaM ma hA b+hu ta pa ti i maM ba ling gr-ih+Na bya hA la sa swAhA/gtor ma'i sngags so//oM dIp+ta lo tsa 
6b4(kha skong): na be kr-i tA na na ma hA TAT+Ta hA sa nA dI naM dIpa ya swAhA//man me'i sngags so//oM dza ya dza ya su dza ya bi shwa da pa tA ra gnyug ma s+tha na kr-i taM ta na ta thA ga ta sha ba sa rdza ya ma ma ka D+haM ka ru swAhA/ces pa gshegs su gsol ba'i 
6b5(kha skong): sngags so||da nas rdo rje 'dzin pa nyid//chos thams cad kyi rjes su 'gro ba dang //ldog pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par 'gags nas/gos sngags yongs su rdzogs pa'i 'phrin las 'di nyid bka' stsal to//
6b6(kha skong): nam mkha'i bskyed pa byang chub gnas par//hUM las yongs su rdzogs pa yi//rdo rje 'jigs byed bskal par bya//dmar po zhal gcig phyag gnyis pa//srin po rang khros pa 'dra//dbyug pa 'od khrag phyag na 
6b7(kha skong): 'dzin/zhes te bkra shis pa/gzhon nu ma yin pa bya ba/gsol man gyi man gyi man ngag/gsang sngags rgyal po 'di bzlas na//'dod pa'i dngos grub thams cad 'byung //nam mkha'i dbyings su dbus 
7a1(kha skong): @#//gnas par//ya yig sngon po las skyes pa'i//khro bo rdo rje 'jigs byed ni//pad+ma nyi ma'i mdud steng na//sems dpa'i skyil krung kyis gnas pa'i//gri gug bi ni pa la

8a1(kha skong): @#//mkha' la khyer//gang dum lag dum ro langs dang //wa dang spyang ki gtum po dang //mkha' 'gro pho mo sha za'i tshogs//bya dang gcan gzan sna tshogs bri//skyi bung rab

8b7(kha skong): kyis lkugs//dug dang khrag dang sgo gang dang //sha chen til mar ra ba sbags te//brgya rtsa brgyad du bsregs byas na//pha rol gzhan gyi gsang sngags gcod//tsan+da dkar po 'bras dkar dag/
9a1(kha skong): @#//til can 'bras can yungs kar dang //ut+pal ka ra ga dung dang //shar du bltas te brgya rtsa brgyad//bsregs na dus rnams kun tu srung //gang zhig bya ba'i rig sogs dang //
9a2(kha skong): sngags rnams bsam gtan ngas ldan pas//snying rje yid ni maN+Dal na//'dod pa thams cad 'dir bsgrub bya//gzhan du rmongs pas byas pa bslu//blo gros med gang shes rab 'tshal//sngags dang dam 
9a3(kha skong): tshig dman gyur cing //ting nge 'dzin la mngon g.yel ba//brtson pa nyung zhing lhod pa 'gas//bskal pa stong du bsgrubs byas kyang //'bras bu yod pa ma yin te//
9a4(kha skong): thang chad pa nyid du chud//dper na blo zhan byis pa rnams//'ga' zhig 'bras 'dod 'khrul gyur nas//phub ma rlung khang byed pa la//'bras yod nam yang mi 'gyur bzhin//ji ltar sbyin dang hUM zla 
9a5(kha skong): dang //dbang po'i gzhu rnams rtag min te//thar pa 'dod pa'i bya ba yang //sgyu ma'i nyid la sgyu ma rol//ye shes gcig pu rnam grol rgyu//stong skad dpag par khros mi nus//de la spyad cing gzhan 
9a6(kha skong): bzod na//brtags pa'i snying po de la min//de la dngos rnams ma grub na//sgrub pa'i rim gzhan smos ci dgos//bya ba dang ni byed pa dag/nges par bde ba dag mi bzod//dam 
9a7(kha skong): grog g.yang sar lhung yang bri'i//tshang pa rtog pa 'di mi bzod//rtog pa nyid kyang rigs min te//dngos po yod par ga la 'gyur//dper na byis pa rnams la shes//sa 'di spong 
9b1(kha skong): dang ma nyid kyis//'bras bu shing thog mar blangs la//khyod la bde bas sbyin zhes brjod//de bzhin rnam pa du ma yang //'jig rten rtog pa tha dad pas//de dag drang bar bya phyir bshad//
9b2(kha skong): bcom ldan lha yi lha mdun du//sha chen po yi zhal zas dang //chang dang til mar ma he khrag/rab tu zhim pa'i zas mchog rnams//dus rnams rtag tu dbul bar bya//gsang sngags rgyal po 
9b3(kha skong): yi ge bcu//bdag gis rgyun tu gdon bya zhing //bsam gtan rtag tu brtson par bya//de ltar shes shing sgrub byed pa'i//skyes bu blo ldan de yis ni//sangs rgyas nyid kyang myur 'grub 'gyur//zhi 
9b4(kha skong): sogs las la smos ci dgos//grong khyer dkrung na sngags pa des//khyi sha sbal he ru sbal sha//rnga mo byis pa'i sha rnams la//stong rtsa brgyad du bsreg par bya//rta sha dri chen rab sbyar nas//ril bu 
9b5(kha skong): byas nas bdag mdun du//gsang sngags rgyal po bzlas byas nas//brgya rtsa brgyad du bsregs pas ni//rgyal po dbang du byed par 'gyur//bud med dbang du byed 'dod na//nya sha chang dang sha chen rnams//
9b6(kha skong): rdo rje tshul sogs byas te//brgya rtsa brgyad du bsre bas par bya//tha mal dbang du byed 'dod na/sha chen gang po de dang bcas//byang chub sems dang bsres byas te//stong phrag brgyad du bsreg par bya/
9b7(kha skong): gzhan dus rnams thams cad du//khyi sha nya shar dud rtsi lnga//cha mnyam rol bu byas nas ni//brgya rtsa brgyad du bsres na 'gugs//sbyin sreg las rnams thams cad kyang //ma he zhal gyi 
10a1(kha skong): @#//sbyor bas ni//gsang sngags rgyal pos 'dod pa sbyar//ma yengs rtse gcig sems kyis bya//gsungs dang ma gsungs mtha' dag kyang //ji lta ba yi rim pa yis//
10a2(kha skong): rgyud kyi rgyal po nyid nyer gnas//sangs rgyas 'bras bu myur du 'thob//zhi la zhi ba'i sems kyis te//rgyal po lta bur rgyas pa la//sdang ba la chags pas dbang la'o//drag po'i khas la khro zhing 
10a3(kha skong): rtsub//rab tu gtum pa'i yid kyis ni//gsod pa'i sems dang ldan par bya//gang zhig ltos pa med pa'i mi//grub pa myur du chol byed na//sems kyi rang bzhin rnam dag pa//rdo rje 'dzin par de 
10a4(kha skong): 'grub 'gyur//dngos po gang dang gang spyod yul//de dang de yi rang bzhin du//mnyam gzhag nyams par mi bya la//dus rnams rtag tu rnal 'byor bas//shin tu mnyam par gzhag par bya//
10a5(kha skong): de ni thar pa nyid kyi rgyu//rgyud kyi rgyal po 'di la ni//'dod pa'i dngos grub myur byed de//dka' ba med pa'i thabs 'di ni//dka' spyod brtul zhugs rnam sngangs te//shin tu bde bar sgrub pa'i thabs//
10a6(kha skong): grub 'jigs med bdud rtsi'i ro//blo ldan legs par bar bya ste//shin tu bsod pas bsgrub pa bya//mi shes blo gros rmongs pa 'ga'//rtog pa'i dra bas bsgrib pa'i tshe//bsod nams dang ni sdig 
10a7(kha skong): pa yi||phung po rnam pa gnyis su brtag/'on kyang 'dod pa thams cad ni//rgyud mchog 'di las 'byung 'gyur phyir//dmar zhing brtson pa chung ba 'ga'//'di la zhugs na myur du 'grub pa dang bas 
10b1(kha skong): ngo mtshar rgyud 'di ni//rnyed dka' khyad par mchog gyur pa//yi ge'i dbyings kyang mi bstan na//bshad cing 'grol ba smos ci dgos//dbang rnams nges par sla bas shing //bla ma mnyes par ma byas tshe//bstan kyang thos 
10b2(kha skong): par mi 'gyur te//tsha ba la sogs 'khor bar 'khor//de skad ston pas bshad pa'i tshe//smra rnams myos shing brgyal rmongs te//kun kyang 'dud cing mgo bslad//'jig rten 'di ni shin tu g.yos//gang zhig rgyud 
10b3(kha skong): 'di nyon pa dang //shes nas 'chang par byed pa dang //dus rnams kun tu 'don byed na//tshe 'dir 'jigs pa kun bsal te//shi nas mtho ris myur du 'thob//gal te gzhan du rgyud mchog 'di//bstan nas 
10b4(kha skong): bshad dam gsal phye na//de ni sdug bsngal mang myos nas//shi nas myur du dmyal bar 'gro//rang gis shin tu bcad bya zhing //dngos grub nyid bzhin mngon byas na//sdug bsngal mtha' dag nges zab nas//
10b5(kha skong): myur du bla med thob par 'gyur//dpal rdo rje 'jigs byed kyi rtog pa'i rgyud kyi rgyal po las/sbyin sreg gi rim par phye ba'i le'u ste bzhi pa'o////dpal ldan rdo rje 'jigs byed kyi rtogs pa'i 
10b6(kha skong): rgyud kyi rgyal po las kyi dngos grub brtag pa zhes bya ba rdzogs so////paN+Di ta chen po a mo g+ha'i zhabs nus pa can dang /bod kyi lo ts+tsha ba mar pa ban+de chos kyi dbang phyug grags pas bsgyur 
10b7(kha skong): cing zhes pa'o//slad kyis grub thob chen po karma shrI'i zhal snga nas mnyan te/khyad pa ko ts+tsha ba nyi ma rgyal mtshan gyis bsgyur cing zhus te gtan la phab pa'o////
11a1(kha skong): @#//rgya gar skad du/Ar+ya su ga pa b+ha stong ga u na tsa ra dzA ba ma ma hA ya na swA hA/bod skad du/'phags pa gser 'od dam par rdo rje'i dbang po'i rgyal po zhes bya ba theg pa chen po'i mdo bam pad shog 
11a2(kha skong): 'das pa dang ma byon pa dang /de ltar byung ba'i sangs rgyas dang /byang chub sems dpa' dang /rang sangs rgyas dang /'phags pa nyan thos thams cad la phyag 'tshal lo//bdag gis thos pa dus gcig na//pu

11a4(kha skong): mnyan na shin tu zab pa dang //yongs su brtags na zab pa nyid//gser 'od dam pa 'di 'chad do//phyogs bzhir sangs rgyas rnams kyis ni//byin gyis brlabs kyis byin brlabs pa//mi 'khrugs pas ni dgang 
11a5(kha skong): phyogs su//rin chen tog gis lho phyogs su//'od dpag med kyis nub phyogs su//rnga sgra yis ni byang phyogs su//sdig pa thams cad gzhig pa'i phyir//bshad pa dam pa bkra shis pa//sdig pa'i las rnams 
11a6(kha skong): zab byed pa//bde ba thams cad sbyin byed pa//sdug bsngal thams cad 'jig byed pa//thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi gzhi//dpal kun gyis ni legs par brgyan//byin gyis brlabs te brjod par bya//sems can 
11a7(kha skong): gang dag dbang po nyams//mdzad pa dang tshe nyams dang //phun sum tshogs min gang chags dang //lha rnams rgyab kyis phyogs pa dang //mdza' sdug skye bo sngar ba dang //ban bza' la sogs gtsos pa dang //phan tshun 
11b1(kha skong): mthun pa ma yin dang //nor ba shing sa pas ni bzhin pa dang //mya ngan las dang //dong dang //'jigs pa dang ni phongs pa dang //gza' dang rgyas dang byad sems dang //mi bzad gdon gyis nyen pa dang /mya ngan ngal las byung ba yi//
11b2(kha skong): rmi lam sdig pa mthong na yang //des ni rab bkrus gtsang ma yis//mdo sde dam pa mnyan par bya//gang dag yid dge sems dang zhing //bas gtsang rnams kyis rab brgyan te//zab mo sangs rgyas spyod yul pa//
11b3(kha skong): mdo 'di nyan par byed na ni//mdo sde 'di yi gzi brjid kyis//srog chags kun gyi gnod pa dang //shin tu mi bzad 'di 'dra ba//de dag rtag tu zhi bar 'gyur//'jig rten skyong ba bdag nyid dang //bon po tshogs kyi 
11b4(kha skong): dbang phyug bcas//gnod sbyin bye ba du ma yis//de dag la ni srung bar byed//lha mo chen mo dbyangs can dang //nai rany+dza nar gnas pa dang //'byung po ma mo 'phrog ma dang //sa yi lha mo brtan ma dang //tshangs pa'i dbang 
11b5(kha skong): dang lha dbang dang //klu dbang mthu chen rnams dang ni//mi 'am ci dbang lha min dbang //de bzhin nam mkha' lding dbang rnams//sde dang stobs dang do zhon pa dang //bcas te de dag de lhags nas//nyin dang mtshan 
11b6(kha skong): mo mi 'phel ba//de dag la ni srung du byed//zab mo sangs rgyas spyod yul gsungs kun gyi gsang ba ste//bskal pa bye bar rnyed dka' ba//mdo 'di rab tu gnas par bya//gang dag mdo 'di nyan pa dang //gang dag gzhan la 
11b7(kha skong): thob byed dang //gang dag rjes su yi rang dang //gang dag mchod pa byed pa rnams//de dag lha dang klu dag dang //mi dang mi 'am ci dag dang //lha min gnod sbyin du ma yis//bskal pa bye bar mchod par 'gyur//
12a1(kha skong): @#//srog chags bsod nams byed pa rnams//de dag bsod nams phung po ni//mtha' yas bcas kyang med par na//bsam gyi/mi spyad skye bar 'gyur/phyogs bcu'i 
12a2(kha skong): pa'i sangs rgyas dang //de bzhin gser spyod pa yi//byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyis kyang //rang dang bzung bar 'du 'dzi yin//gtsang ma'i gos rnams rta bab gos la//dri zhim pa la gos 'chang zhing //

12a4(kha skong): gyis||gang dag man 'di nyan pa na//de dag mi nang legs par 'ongs//mi srid pa la gsang bar thob//'chi ba 'dod pas 'chi bar 'gyur//gang gi rna ba'i bu gar yang //bshad pa 'di ni sangs rgyas 
12a5(kha skong): 'gyur ro//de dag dge ba spangs pa ste//sangs rgyas mang pos rab tu bsngags////gser 'od dam pa mdo sde'i dbang po'i rgyal po las glang bzhi'i dbu zhos bya bu ste dang po'o//

12b1(kha skong): zhig thub ste/'di ltar sku tshe thung snyam nas/de yang 'di snyam du sems te/bcom ldan 'das kyis kyang 'di skad du rgyud gnyis dang rkyen gnyis kyis tshe ring bar 'gyur te/gnyis gang zhe na 'di lta ste/srog gcod 
12b2(kha skong): pa spangs pa dang //pha zas rab tu sbyin pa'o//zhes gsungs te/bcom ldan 'das shAkya thub pas ni bskal pa bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong phra rab mdor mang po zhes srog gcod pa spangs par gyur pa nas/dge ba

13a1(kha skong): @#//lho phyogs nas ni de bzhin gshegs pa rin chen tog byung //nub phyogs nas ni de bzhin bshegs pa tshe dpal med byung /byang phyogs nas ni de bzhin gzigs pa rnga sgra byung ste/seng ge'i khrid dag

13a3(kha skong): bar dang /phyogs bcu kun tu 'jig rten gyi khams kyi bar dang /phyogs bcu kunatu 'jig rten gyi khams gang+ga'i klung gi bye ma snyed kyi bar dag snang bas khyab par gyur/lha'i me tog dag gi char yang bab/
13a4(kha skong): lha'i sil stan gyi sgra ming byung ngo//sangs rgyas kyi mthus stong gsum gyi stong chen po 'i 'jig rten gyi khams 'dir sems can thams cad kyang lha'i bde ba dang ldan par gyur te/sems can dbang po ma tshad kyang

13b1(kha skong): chos rnams rgya cher byung bar gyur te//de nas byang chub sems dpa' mdzes pa'i tog gis zhabs rgyas bcom ldan 'das de dag mthong nas ngo mtshar rmad du gyur te/de tshe bzhing mgu la yi rangs shing rab tu dga' ste/
13b2(kha skong): dka' ba dang yid bde bskyes nas/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das ded ga gal ba de logs su thal mo sbyar ba btud nas/sangs rgyas bcom ldang 'das de dag rnams rjes su dran la bcom ldan 'das shAkya thub

13b6(kha skong): dang bcas pa'i 'jig rten dang /bdud dang bcas pa dang /tshangs pa dang bcas pa dang /dge sbyor dang bran zer bcas pa'i skye dgu dang /lha dang mi dang lcam yin du bcas pa'i nang nas/bcom ldan 'das de bzhin 
13b7(kha skong): gshegs pa shAkya thub pa'i sku tshe'i tshad kyi mtha' phyi ma'i mtha'i mA'i bar du yang rtogs par nus pa de da kyis su yang ma mthong ngo//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das de dag gis de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku tshe'i 
14a1(kha skong): @#//tshad bshad pa brjod ma thag tu de nas de'i tshe sangs rgyas kyis mthus/lha'i bu 'dod pa na spyod pa rnams dang /gzugs na spyod pa rnams dang /klu dang gnod sbyin dang dri za dang /lha ma yin dang /nam mkha'i sdid 
14a2(kha skong): dang /mi 'am ci dang /lto phye chen po'i bar dag dang /byang chub sems dpa' bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong phrag du ma 'dus nas/byang chub sems dpa' mdzes pa'i tog gi khyim der lhags so//de nas de bzhin 
14a3(kha skong): gshegs pa de dag gis thams cad dang ldan pa'i 'khor de la/bcom ldan 'das shAkya thub pa'i sku tshe'i tshad bshad pa la tshogs su bcad de mdor bka' stsal pa rgya mtsho'i chu rnams thams cad kyi//snyigs pa dag ni 
14a4(kha skong): bgrang bar nus//shAkya thub pa'i sku tshe'i tshang //sus kyang bgrang bar mi nus so//ri rab rnams ni thams cad kyang /phra rab rdul du bgrang bar nus//shAkya thug pa'i sku tshe'i tshad//sus kyang bgrang bar sa nus so//sa la gar ram

14b1(kha skong): cig/rgyal ba'i sku tshe 'di tha ma ni//grangs ni gang du rab rib mi dmigs so//de nas de'i tshe 'khor der slob dpon lung ston pa bram ze ko di n+ya zhes bya ba dang /bram ze stong khrig bum pa bcom ldan 'das la mchod pa byas te/

14b3(kha skong): 'das sems can thams cad la thugs brtse ba/thugs rje chen po dang ldan pa/phan par bzhed pa/sems can thams cad la pha ma lta bu/mi mnyam pa dang mnyam par gyur pa/zla ba lta bur snang bar

14b5(kha skong): cang mi gsung bar gyur to//de nas sangs rgyas kyi mthus 'khor der lits+tsha bI gzhon nu sems can thams cad kyis mthong na dga' ba zhes bya ba de spobs pa skyes nas/slob dpon lung ston pa kau di n+ya la 'di skad 
14b6(kha skong): ces smras so//ci dril dri chen po khyod bcom ldan 'das la dam pa cig gsol lam/bdag gis khyod la dam pa cig sbyin no//kham zes smras pa/lits+tsha bI gzhon nu bdag ni bcos la mchod pa dang /ring bsrel gyi bye ma 
14b7(kha skong): 'dos pa'i phyir bcom ldan 'das kyi bar dang brel yungs 'bru tsam la bskyed par 'dod do//ring bsrel yungs 'bru tsam de ma zho bar mchod na/sum cu rtsa gsum pa'i lha rnams kyi bdag po 'dra'i zhig 'thob ces grags so//phyi 'di 
15a1(kha skong): @#//pI gzhon nu gser 'od dam pa'i mdo can thos rabs sangs rgyas thams cad kyis shes par dka' ba/khong du chud par dka' ba/de lta bu'i mtshan nyid dang /yon tan dang ldan pas med 
15a2(kha skong): 'od dam pa'i mdo 'chad ces khrag gis nyon cig/kye yi ts+tsha bI gzhon nu gser 'od ngam pa'i mdo 'di ni de ltar shes par dka' bas na/kho bo cag mtha' 'khob kyi gling gi bram ze rnams 
15a3(kha skong): ni gang bcangs pa na/sems can rnams myur du sum cu rtsa gsum pa'i bdag po thob par 'gyur ba'i ring bsrel yungs 'bru tsam za ma tog gi nang du bcug la bcangs na rung ngo//kye li ts+tsha bI gzhon nu khyod ci 
15a4(kha skong): de bzhin gshegs pa las ring bsrel yungs 'bru tsham gsol nas/ring bsrel za ma tog du bcug te bcangs pas/sems can rnams sum cu rtsa gsum pa'i bdan po thob par mi 'byod dang /kye li tsA k+Shi 
15a5(kha skong): gzhon nu kho bos ni dam pa de lta bu gsol to//de nas li ts+tsha bI gzhon nu'ajig rten thams cad kyis mthong na dga' bas slob dpon lung ston pa bram ze god kyi la tshigs su bcang pa dam gyis smad k+Sha sa la/nam 
15a6(kha skong): zhig gang kA'i chu rgyun la//me tog rnams ni skye ba dang //bya rog dmar por 'byung ba dang //khu byug dung mdog 'drar 'gyur dang //'dzum+bur ta la'i 'bru chags dang //'bra gor a smu'i dag pa chags pa//de yi 
15a7(kha skong): na yungs 'bru tsam//ring bsrel du ni gsal bar 'gyur//dar zhig ngu smra ba spu rnams pas//gos su legs par btags gyur te//dgun gyi grang bsel byed pa//de tshe ring bsrel yod par 'gyur//
15b1(kha skong): nam zhig sbrang bu'i rkang lag las//yang thog legs par byas gyur te//brtan zhing rab tu mi g.yo ba//de tshe ring bsrel yod par 'gyur//nam zhig srin bu pad ma kun//so ni dkar por skyes gyur te/rna zhing 
15b2(kha skong): chen por gyur pa na//de tshe ring bsrel yong par 'gyur//mtho ris su ni 'dzegs pa'i phyir//nam zhig ri bong rwa rnams las//skas ni legs par byas gyur pa//de tshe ring bsrel yodapar 'gyur//nam zhig 
15b3(kha skong): skas ni der 'dzegs te//byi bas zla ba bza' ba dang //sgra gcan la yang gnod byed pa//de tshe ring bsrel yid par 'gyur//grong na rgyu ba'i sbrang bu yis//nam zhig rdza ma'i char 'thungs te//khyim na gnas par 
15b4(kha skong): byed 'gyur ba//de tshe ring bsrel yang par 'gyur//nam zhig bong bu der gyur la//mchu dmar bim pa 'drar gyur cing //glu gar rnams la mkhas gyur pa//de tshe ring bsril yod par 'gyur//nam
15b5(kha skong): zhig 'ug pa bya rog rnams//dben par dong nas rtse ba dang //phan tshun mthun par gyur pa na//de tshe ring? bsrel yod par 'gyur//pa la sha yi lo ma rnams//rin chen sna tshogs gdugs gyur te//char 'bar?
15b6(kha skong): skyabs su nam gyur pa//de tshe ring bsrol yod par 'gyur//nam zhig rgya mtsho'i gru bo che//'khrul 'khor dang ni g.yor mor bcas//thang la byung ste 'gro ba na//de tshe ring bsrel yod par 'gyur//nam zhig bya ni 'ug
15b7(kha skong): pa yis//ri bo spos kyi bad ldan pa//mchal thogs te 'gro gyur ba//de tshe ring bsrol yong bar 'gyur//slob dpon lung ston pa bram ze ka di n+yas tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag thos nas/lits+tsha bI gzhon nu 'dzag?
16a1(kha skong): @#//thams cad kyis mthong na dga' ba la tshigs su bcad pa dag gis slar smras pa/legs so legs so gzhon nu mchog/rgyal ba'i sras po tshig po che//dpa' zhing thabs la mos ldan pa//
16a2(kha skong): lung bstan pa ni dam pa thob//'jig rten mgon po skyob pa po//de bzhin gshegs pa'i che ba nyid//bsam gyis mi khyab go rim bzhin//gzhon nus bdag la mnyan par gyis//sangs rgyas yul ni bsam mi khyab//
16a3(kha skong): de bzhin gshegs rnams mtshungs pa med//sangs rgyas thams cad rtag tu zhi//sangs rgyas thams cad yang dag byung //sangs rgyas thams cad kha dog mtshungs//'di ni sangs rgyas chos nyid min//bcom ldan 'di 
16a4(kha skong): ni bcos ma min//de bzhin gshegs ngam skyes te//rdo rje lta bur smra ba'i sku//sprul pa'i sku ni ston par mdzad//drang srong chen po'i ring bsrel ni//yungs 'bru tsam zhes bya ba med//sprul rus pa khrag med na//
16a5(kha skong): ring bsrel yod par ga la 'gyur//sems can rnams la phan pa'i phyir//thabs kyis ring bsrel bzhag par mdzad//rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas chos kyi sku//de bzhin gshegs pa chos kyi dbyings//chos bsam 
16a6(kha skong): mdzad pa ci 'dra ba//bcom ldan 'das kyi sku de 'dra//bdag gis 'di thos shes nas ni//bdag gis dam pa gsol ba byas//de nyid kho na lung bstan phyir//gtam gsal ni bdag gis byas//de nas lha'i lus 
16a7(kha skong): khri nyid stong gis/de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku tshe'i tshad bstan pa zab mo de lta bu dag thos nas/thams cad kyis bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu sems bskyed de/de dag med kyi kun tu rtogs pa 
16b1(kha skong): drag tu dga' nas sgra dbyangs gcig tu tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//sangs rgyas mya ngan yongs mi 'da'//chos kyang nub par mi 'gyur te//sems can yongs su smin mdzad phyir//yongs su mya ngan 'da' ba ston//
16b2(kha skong): sangs rgyas bcom ldan bsam mi khyab//de bzhin gshegs pa rtag pa'i sku//sems can rnams la phan pa'i phyir//bkod pa rnam pa sna tshogs ston//de nas byang chub sems dpa' mdzes pa'i tog gis 
16b3(kha skong): sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das de dag dang /skyes bu dam pa de gnyis las//bcom ldan 'das shAkya thub pa'i sku tshe'i tshad bstan pa thos nas/tshim zhing mgu la yi rangs shing rab tu dga' ba dang /yid bde 
16b4(kha skong): ba skyes nas dga' ba dang /mgu ba rgya chen pos khyab par gyur to//de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku tshe'i tshad bstan ba 'di bshad pa'i tshe/sems can grangs med pa dpag tu med pa dag/bla na med pa yang dag par 
16b5(kha skong): rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu sems skyes so//de bzhin gshegs pa de dag kyang mi snang bar gyur to//gser 'od dam pa mdo sde'i dbang po'i rgyal po las de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku cho'i tshad bstan pa'i le'u 
16b6(kha skong): nas bya ste gnyis pa'o////
17a1(kha skong): @#//mas yongs su bskor te/mdun gyis bltas nas chos sngon pa mthong ngo//de nas yang skyes bu bram ze'i gzugs lta bu zhig rnga de rdung ba mthong ste/rnga'i sgra de las bstan pa 
17a2(kha skong): tshigs su bcad pa 'di lta bu dag byung ba thos so//de nas byang chub sems dpa' ma nges pa'i tog gnyid mang ma thag tu/bstan pa'i tshigs su bcad pa de dag rjes su dran no//rjes su dran nas kyang de'i mtshan mo 
17a3(kha skong): zad pa dang /rgyal po'i khab kyi grong khyer chen po nas byung te/srog chags stong phrag du ma dang lhan cig tu bya rgod kyi phung po'i rig la ba dang /bcom ldan 'das ga la ba der song ste phyin nas/bcom ldan

17a5(kha skong): bcom ldan 'das ga la bde logs su thal mo sbyar ba btud nas rmi lam na gang rnga'i sgra las bstan pa'i tshigs su bcad ba thos pa de dag gsol to//gser 'od dam pa mdo sde'i dbang po'i rgyal po las rmi lam 
17a6(kha skong): mthong ba'i le'u zhes bya ste gsum pa'o////nub gcig g.yol ba med par yang //bdag gi rmi lam gyur pa ni//mdzes pa'i rnga chen mthong ba ste//thams cad du ni gser gyi 
17a7(kha skong): bon//ji ltar nyi ma bzhin du 'bar//kun tu rnam par lhang nge ste//phyogs bcu rnams su snang bar 'gyur//kun tu rin chen shing drung na//rin chen bai DUr+ya dag gi/khri la sangs rgyas rnams 
17b1(kha skong): bzhugs te//brgya stong phrag ni du ma yi//'khor dgu ba mdun gyis bltas pa mthong //bram ze'i gzugs ni 'dra ba nyid//tshan rab rnga ba mthong //de yis de la brdungs pa dang //tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag byung//
17b2(kha skong): gser 'od dam pa'i rnga chen sgra yis ni//stong gsum 'jig rten 'jig rten gsum 'di na//ngan song sdug bsngal gshan rtsa'i spag bsngal dang //dbul ba'i sdug bsngal ma lus zhi bar shog/rnga bo che yi sgra skad

17b4(kha skong): dbang po//'phags pa'i yon tan kun dang ldan pa ltar//skye dgu ting 'dzin byang chub yan lag gi/yon tan ldan zhing yon tan rgya mtshor gyur//rnga bo che yi sgra skad 'di yis ni//sems can thams cad 
17b5(kha skong): tshangs pa'i dbyangs ldan gyur//sangs rgyas byang chub dam pa mchog rig shog/chos kyi 'khor lo dge bar bskor gyur cig/bskal ba bsam gyis mi khyab bzhugs par shog/'gro la phan re chos 
17b6(kha skong): kyang ston gyur cig/nyon mongs 'joms gyur sdug bsngal sel bar shog/'dod chags zhe sdang gti mug zhi gyur cig/sems can gang dag ngan song sar gnas te//rus pa'i lus la me lce rang 'bar 
17b7(kha skong): ba//de dag rnga chen bsgrags pa thos gyur cig/sangs rgyas phyag 'tshal lo zhes tshig rtos shog/sems can kun gyis skye ba brgya dag dang //skye ba stong phrag bye ba skye dran shog/thub pa'i dbang po rtag tu dran gyur 
18a1(kha skong): @#//cig/de dag gis ni rgya chen gsung thos shog/rnga bo che yi sgra skad 'di yis ni//sangs rgyas rnams dang rtag tu 'grogs rnyed gyur//sdig pa'i las rnams rnam par spong bar

20b1(kha skong): dang //zhe sdang gti mug mun pa nyam nga dang //dbang ba'i nyam nga dus kyi nyam nga dang //bsod nams sgrub pa nyam nga dag gis kyang //rgyal ba mi nyam mngon sum mchis nas ni//sdig pa de kun so sor bshags par bgyi//
20b2(kha skong): sangs rgyas gser mchog ri snang ri rab 'dra//yon tan rgya mtsho 'dra la phyag 'tshal lo//rgyal ba de dag rnams la skyabs su mchi//rgyal ba de dag kun la mgos phyag 'tshal//gser mdog gser ni dri ma med pa'i gser

21b1(kha skong): mug zhi bar bgyi//skye ba brgya dang skye ba bye ba stong //bdag ni rtag tu skye ba dran par bgyi//thub pa'i dbang po rtag tu dran gyur cig/de yi gsung ni rgya chen mos par bgyi//bdag ni dge ba'i las rnams 'di dag gis//rtag 
21b2(kha skong): tu sangs rgyas 'grogs pa rnyed par bgyi//sdig pa'i las rnams rnam par spang bar bgyi//dge ba'i 'byung gnas dge rnams spyad par bgyi//zhing rnams kun na srog chags thams cad kyang //'jig rten dag na sdug bsngal

23a1(kha skong): pa 'di yis ni//bskal pa drug cur ngan song spang bar 'gyur//tshigs su bcad pa brje ba 'di dag gis skyes pa dung min bram ze rgyal rigs las//'phags pa brgya pa drug ste rgyal ba gang bstod pa//
23a2(kha skong): tshe rabs kun tu skye ba dran par 'gyur//yan lag dbang po kun gyis yan lag brgyan//bsod nams yon tan sna tshogs rnams dang ldan//mi dang rgyal pos rtag tu da mchod do//de dang de dag tu ni de 'drar 'gyur//gang 
23a3(kha skong): dag rna ba'i du gar bshags pa 'di ni grags byung ba//de dag sangs rgyas gcig gis gnas nas dge ba byas ma yin//gnyis la ma yin bzhi la ma yin lnga min bcu la min//de ltar sangs rgyas stong 
23a4(kha skong): gi gan nas dge ba byas ma yin//gser bod dam pa mdo sde'i dbang po'i rgyal po las//bshags pa'i le'u zhes bya ste bzhi pa'o////
23b1(kha skong): phyungs rnams kun gyis sangs rgyas gsung snyan te//tshangs pa'i sgra dbyangs rab tu bsgrags pa'i gsung //bung ba rma bya ut+pal sngon po'i dbu//mthon mthing lcang lo bya ni tsa sha 'dra//khab dung ltar rab tu dkar po'i 
23b2(kha skong): tshems//rtag tu gser ltar rnam par mdzes par gda'o//spyan ni ring zhing mthon mthing dri ma med//ut+pal sngon po rab tu rgyas pa 'dra//ljags ni che zhing legs la pad+ma'i mdog/pad+ma 'drar snang pad+ma'i ze ba 'dra//
23b3(kha skong): zhal gyi mdzod spu pad rtsa dung dang 'dra//bai DUr+ya'i mdog 'dra g.yas su 'khyil//thub pa'i zla ba phra zhing zad pa 'dra//sku yi lte ba bung ba gsal ba bzhin//zhal mtho'i shangs ni rtag tu lhag ga ste//'jam zhing sa le 
23b4(kha skong): gser ltar kha dog bzang //rtag par rgyun du rgyal ba thams cad kyi//ro mchog 'jam zhing dam pa mchog rab 'phags//spu yi bu ga nas ni re re skyes//spu rnams skyes pa g.yas su 'khyil ba ste//
23b5(kha skong): mthon mthing 'od 'bar 'tsher zhing lcang lo can//rma bya'i mgrin sdug 'dra bar sngo la mdzes//bltams ma thag tu sku yis snang bar bgyis//phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten dag ni thams cad gang //'jig rten gsuM 
23b6(kha skong): na sdug bsngal mtha' yas zhi//bde ba kun gyis sems can rnams kyang tshim//sems can dmyal 'gro dud 'gro'i 'gro ba dang //yi dgas lha gnas mi yi 'gro rnams su//sems can de dag thams cad bde bas 
23b7(kha skong): tshim//ngan song 'gro ba dang gnas can zhi//kha dog mdog bzang bseng dang 'dra ba'i 'od//btso ma'i gser ltar rab tu sdang ba'i skye//bde gshegs zla ba rab tu dri med zhal//'dzum zhing rnam mdzes shin tu 
24a1(kha skong): dri med zhal//gzhon nu bcos 'drar sku lus yan lag 'jam//seng ge 'dra bar rtsal gyis gnon pa'i mchog/phyag ring phyag ni rab tu ring ba ste//sA la'i lcug ma rlung gis

24a3(kha skong): bas//zhing rnams mtha' yas brgya stong rnams su yang //mtshan rgyad zla ba nyi ma mang po dang //de dag thams cad 'od ni med par 'gyur//sangs rgyas nyi ma 'jig rten sgron ma ste//sangs rgyas nyi ma brgya phrag

24a6(kha skong): dang //gang dag phra ba'i rngul snyed bzhugs pa yi//rgyal ba de dag la ri rab dang bzhi//ngag yid lus kyis mchog dbul ba dang //spos rnams dbul dang bsngags pa brgya dag dang //dga' ba'i sems kyis kyang ni phyag 
24a7(kha skong): 'tshal lo//rgyal ba'i yon tan gang dag dger gyur pa//snying po chos rab sna tshogs du ma ste//bdag gis bskal pa stong du lte brgya yis//sangs rgyas yon tan brjod par mi nus so//rgyal ba gcig gis gong 
24b1(kha skong): tan rnams kyang ni//lce stong gis kyang cung zad brjod mi nus//bdag gis rgyal ba nga ni thams cad kyi//yon tan gcig rgyas brjod pa'i nga la nus//lhar bcas 'jig rten thams cad mchod gyur te//srid pa'i rtse mo

24b3(kha skong): bsags pa//des ni o rgyal ba reg gyur cig/de ltar rgyal pos sangs rgyas rnams bstod nas//rgyal po 'di ltar smon lam 'debs pa ni//ma 'ongs bskal pa mtha' yas rnams su yang //gang zhing gang du bdag 
24b4(kha skong): ni skye ba na//rmi lam du ni 'di 'dra'i rnga blta zhing //der ni bshags pa 'di 'dra mnyan par bgyi//rgyal bstod pad+ma 'byung rgyal 'di 'dra ba//skye ba de dang der ni rnyed par bgyi//sangs rgyas yon tan mtha' yas mtshungs 
24b5(kha skong): med dang //gang yang bskal pa steng du rnyed dka' ba//de dag rmi lam du yang mnyan par bgyi//nyin gyur tshe yang de dag bshad par bgyi//sems can sdug bsngal mtsho las thar bar bgyi//pha rol phyin pa drug po rdzogs 
24b6(kha skong): par bgyi//phyi nas byang chub bla med thob par bgyi//bdag gi zhing ni lhod pa med gyur cig/rnga bu la rnam par smin pa'i 'bras bu dang //rgyal ba thams cad la yang bstod pa'i rgyus//shAkya thub dbang mngon du blta bar
